- **mass-cytometry-pseudotime-inference**: Use when a proteomic (CyTOF/mass cytometry) time-series single-cell dataset annotated with sampling-day labels needs pseudotime ordering, or when benchmarking whether lazy-teleporting MCMC (memory) improves pseudotime over TI baselines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/mass-cytometry-pseudotime-inference))
- **mass-dataset-feature-annotation**: Use when LC-MS feature tables and compound annotation results (annotate_tbl) from a tidymass/mass_dataset workflow need per-feature MSI level assignment (1-3) or annotation-based filtering, e.g. to keep only MS2-confirmed metabolites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-dataset-feature-annotation))
- **mass-dataset-interoperability-conversion**: Use when a mass_dataset object produced by tidyMass must be consumed by tools outside the tidyMass ecosystem, or when downstream analysis requires SummarizedExperiment, mzTab-M, or MetDNA-compatible files. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-dataset-interoperability-conversion))
- **mass-dataset-metadata-synchronization**: Use when building or modifying an R metabolomics data container that must keep sample metadata and variable metadata rows aligned with a feature (metabolic feature) intensity matrix after subsetting or filtering operations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-dataset-metadata-synchronization))
- **mass-dataset-object-construction**: Use when LC–MS untargeted metabolomics raw data (e.g. mzML files) have been peak-picked/grouped with massprocesser or a cleaned feature table exists, and downstream tidyMass steps (massCleaner, massstat, metpath, metid) require the unified mass_dataset object. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-dataset-object-construction))
- **mass-decimal-digit-integer-conversion**: Use when feeding mass_peak values (e.g. from GAME/examples/MW, such as 286.24-478.41 Da for Cuscuta chinensis) into the csccp-solver-cli DP solver, or when benchmarking how runtime scales with mass precision D=1-5. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-decimal-digit-integer-conversion))
- **mass-decimal-precision-parameterization**: Use when solving CSCCP structure-elucidation tasks with csccp-solver-cli or G.A.M.E. and a targeted molecular weight must be matched at a chosen decimal precision (dec 1–5), balancing runtime cost against mass-spectrum resolution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-decimal-precision-parameterization))
- **mass-defect-based-feature-grouping**: Use when you have a blank-filtered feature table from PFAS LC-HRMS/MS data and want to discover homologous PFAS series (e.g., CF2 ladders) without a suspect list, including series lacking MS/MS corroboration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-defect-based-feature-grouping))
- **mass-defect-based-metabolite-bridging**: Use when you have LC-MS feature lists for in vivo metabolites plus a prototype (in vitro parent component) list from PATBS non-targeted identification, and need to bridge metabolites to prototypes and characterize metabolites across biological matrices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-defect-based-metabolite-bridging))
- **mass-defect-calculation**: Use when processing feature lists from LC- or GC-HRMS data (in mzML format or as custom feature tables with m/z and molecular formula columns) and you need to flag potential PFAS candidates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-defect-calculation) · 3 copies)
- **mass-defect-filtering**: Use when a blank-filtered LC-HRMS feature table from a fluorine-rich sample (e.g. AFFF) must be narrowed to probable PFAS using exact mass matching, mass defect windows, and CF2 homologous-series evidence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-defect-filtering))
- **mass-defect-filtering**: Use when after MS-Dial peak picking and feature table construction, when you observe a high proportion of features with anomalous m/z decimal values that are inconsistent with known metabolite ionization patterns. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-defect-filtering) · 2 copies)
- **mass-defect-filtering-for-chemical-validity**: Use when after loading an MS-DIAL peak list (feature table with m/z, retention time, intensity, and sample assignments) when you need to remove non-organic or chemically implausible features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-defect-filtering-for-chemical-validity) · 2 copies)
- **mass-defect-isotope-annotation**: Use when a preprocessed spatial metabolomics peak table (X, Y coordinates plus m/z ion columns) needs isotope annotation to assess ion-detection reliability, e.g. when computing the isotopic peak ratio quality metric. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-defect-isotope-annotation))
- **mass-defect-mz-error-weighting**: Use when annotating features from AIF LC-MS metabolomics data and you must score/rank candidate metabolites per feature against a fragment library, combining m/z accuracy with pseudo-MS/MS fragment-match specificity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-defect-mz-error-weighting))
- **mass-defect-per-carbon-prioritization**: Use when a post-FeatureFinding feature list (e.g., external_feature_list.xlsx from mzML data) must be reduced from thousands of matrix components to a small set of potential PFAS candidates in non-target screening. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-defect-per-carbon-prioritization))
- **mass-defect-ppm-estimation-via-gaussian-kde**: Use when raw scan-level mass-spectral data (m/z, intensity, retention time per scan) and detected TIC peak boundaries are available and a dataset-specific centWave ppm parameter must be estimated for XCMS/MZmine2 peak picking, without iterative parameter sweeping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-defect-ppm-estimation-via-gaussian-kde))
- **mass-defect-range-optimization**: Decision support for tuning the mass-defect tolerance in MDRB prototype-metabolite bridging when matching unpredictable metabolites and needing to trade off pathway coverage against false positives. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-defect-range-optimization))
- **mass-delete-guard**: Stop one user, bug or leaked key from wiping a Supabase/Postgres table, and recover fast if it already happened: incident steps, delete audit log, soft delete, owner-only delete policies and a delete rate limit. Use after a mass-delete incident or before launch with real user data. ([getshipready/ship-ready-skills](https://github.com/getshipready/ship-ready-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/mass-delete-guard))
- **mass-delta-computation**: Use when when you have parsed two or more MS/MS spectra (precursor m/z and fragment ion lists) and need to quantify all pairwise mass differences between fragment ions before alignment or similarity scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-delta-computation) · 2 copies)
- **mass-deploy**: Use when a user requests a mass deploy, deploy everything or all remote hosts, a full fleet rollout, or a fleet-wide Colmena deployment of this NixOS repository. ([fred-drake/nix](https://github.com/fred-drake/nix/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/mass-deploy) · ★28)
- **mass-deploy-ux**: UX patterns for desktop tools that commission or configure device fleets in parallel — status grids, error triage, resumable worklists, LED locate, throughput ETA. Use when designing a UI that tracks 50+ parallel device operations. ([NerdBase-by-Stark/claude-code-skills](https://github.com/NerdBase-by-Stark/claude-code-skills/tree/HEAD/mass-deploy-ux) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **mass-desire-angle-finder**: Find untapped ad angles for any winning product using deep Reddit/forum research. Input your product + desire + avatar, get a full research report and 5+ battle-tested angles with hooks, outlines, and test priority. ([karausab590-ops/clawads-marketing-skills](https://github.com/karausab590-ops/clawads-marketing-skills/tree/HEAD/mass-desire-angle-finder) · ★13)
- **mass-difference-adduct-annotation**: Use when a pre-processed MALDI-MSI peak table (plain-text CSV exported from SCiLS or MSiReader, or a binned Cardinal MSProcessedImagingExperiment) needs putative adduct annotation, and you want to recover abundant Δmass peaks such as 21.985 and 37.955 Da from pairwise peak differences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-difference-adduct-annotation))
- **mass-difference-adduct-matching**: Use when after correlation-based feature pairing has identified feature groups with matching temporal intensity profiles through direct-injection or plasma ionization mass spectrometry experiments. You have a set of putative feature-pair candidates and need to assign specific chemical identities (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-difference-adduct-matching))
- **mass-difference-calculation-and-matching**: Use when you have centroided data-dependent acquisition (DDA) MS2 spectra from LC- or GC-HRMS and need to annotate detected features with PFAS-specific diagnostic fragments. Use it after feature detection (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-difference-calculation-and-matching) · 2 copies)
- **mass-difference-calculation-from-spectral-peaks**: Use when you have a list of detected masses (m/z peaks) from MALDI-MS imaging data and want to systematically search for adduct relationships. Apply this skill when you suspect that observed peaks include not just parent metabolites but also their adducts with matrix ions (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-difference-calculation-from-spectral-peaks) · 2 copies)
- **mass-difference-edge-annotation**: Use when a raw MS/MS peak list must be transformed into a de novo-style spectrum graph whose nodes carry mass/intensity and edges carry mass differences, as required before Popitam-style graph parsing.
